Rook on f6 attacks f-file. It does NOT attack g7.
So g7 is safe.
If K moves to h6, is h6 attacked?
Pawn on g6 attacks f7 and h7. It does NOT attack h6.
Rook on f6 attacks f-file. It does NOT attack h6.
So h6 is safe.
Between g7 and h6, g7 is generally more active.
Wait, looking at the board layout again:
⭘ ♕ ⭘ ♕ ♕ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘
⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ♚
⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ♖ ♙ ⭘
Usually, the King shouldn't move to the edge if not necessary.
However, h7h6 is moving towards the edge (h-file) but moving down the board.
h7g7 is moving towards the center.
I'll go with h7g7.
